PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain logistics)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    city location locatable - object
    package vehicle - locatable
    truck airplane - vehicle
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?obj - locatable ?loc - location)
    (in ?pkg - package ?veh - vehicle)
    (in_city ?loc - location ?city - city)
    (is_airport ?loc - location)
  )

  (:action load_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in ?p ?t))
  )

  (:action unload_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in ?p ?t) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in ?p ?t)))
  )

  (:action load_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in ?p ?a))
  )

  (:action unload_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in ?p ?a) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in ?p ?a)))
  )

  (:action drive_truck
    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - location ?to - location ?c - city)
    :precondition (and (at ?t ?from) (in_city ?from ?c) (in_city ?to ?c))
    :effect (and (not (at ?t ?from)) (at ?t ?to))
  )

  (:action fly_airplane
    :parameters (?a - airplane ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?a ?from) (is_airport ?from) (is_airport ?to))
    :effect (and (not (at ?a ?from)) (at ?a ?to))
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem logistics_problem)
  (:domain logistics)
  (:objects
    city_0 city_1 - city
    location_0_0 location_1_0 - location
    package_0 - package
    truck_0 truck_1 - truck
    airplane_0 airplane_1 - airplane
  )

  (:init
    (is_airport location_0_0)
    (is_airport location_1_0)
    (in_city location_0_0 city_0)
    (in_city location_1_0 city_1)
    (at package_0 location_1_0)
    (at airplane_0 location_1_0)
    (at airplane_1 location_1_0)
    (at truck_0 location_0_0)
    (at truck_1 location_1_0)
  )

  (:goal
    (at package_0 location_0_0)
  )
)
</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain logistics)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    city location locatable - object
    package vehicle - locatable
    truck airplane - vehicle
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?obj - locatable ?loc - location)
    (in ?pkg - package ?veh - vehicle)
    (in_city ?loc - location ?city - city)
    (is_airport ?loc - location)
  )

  (:action load_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in ?p ?t))
  )

  (:action unload_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in ?p ?t) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in ?p ?t)))
  )

  (:action load_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in ?p ?a))
  )

  (:action unload_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in ?p ?a) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in ?p ?a)))
  )

  (:action drive_truck
    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - location ?to - location ?c - city)
    :precondition (and (at ?t ?from) (in_city ?from ?c) (in_city ?to ?c))
    :effect (and (not (at ?t ?from)) (at ?t ?to))
  )

  (:action fly_airplane
    :parameters (?a - airplane ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?a ?from) (is_airport ?from) (is_airport ?to))
    :effect (and (not (at ?a ?from)) (at ?a ?to))
  )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem logistics_problem)
  (:domain logistics)
  (:objects
    city_0 city_1 - city
    location_0_0 location_1_0 - location
    package_0 - package
    truck_0 truck_1 - truck
    airplane_0 airplane_1 - airplane
  )

  (:init
    (is_airport location_0_0)
    (is_airport location_1_0)
    (in_city location_0_0 city_0)
    (in_city location_1_0 city_1)
    (at package_0 location_1_0)
    (at airplane_0 location_1_0)
    (at airplane_1 location_1_0)
    (at truck_0 location_0_0)
    (at truck_1 location_1_0)
  )

  (:goal
    (at package_0 location_0_0)
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
To transport package_0 from its initial location to the target destination, the following sequence of actions will be performed:

**Step 1: Load the package onto the airplane**
At the airport in location_1_0, load package_0 into airplane_1. Both the package and the airplane are currently at this location, allowing for a successful transfer.
*   **Time:** 00:00 (Start)

**Step 2: Fly the package to the destination city**
Fly airplane_1 from the airport at location_1_0 (city_1) to the airport at location_0_0 (city_0). 
*   **Time:** 01:00

**Step 3: Unload the package at the destination**
Upon arrival at location_0_0, unload package_0 from airplane_1. The package is now at its final destination, fulfilling the goal.
*   **Time:** 02:00 (Completion)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, package_1 is at location_0_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0 and package_1 is at location_1_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

Based on the provided PDDL plan and specification, here is the step-by-step sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: Fly airplane_0 from city_1 to city_0**  
(Action: `fly_airplane airplane_0 location_1_0 location_0_0`)  
**Timestamp:** 0  
Fly airplane_0 from its starting position at the airport in city_1 (location_1_0) to the airport in city_0 (location_0_0).

**Step 2: Load package_1 into airplane_0**  
(Action: `load_airplane package_1 airplane_0 location_0_0`)  
**Timestamp:** 1  
At location_0_0, load package_1 (which is currently at that location) into airplane_0.

**Step 3: Fly airplane_0 back to city_1**  
(Action: `fly_airplane airplane_0 location_0_0 location_1_0`)  
**Timestamp:** 2  
Fly airplane_0, now carrying the package, from the airport in city_0 (location_0_0) back to the airport in city_1 (location_1_0).

**Step 4: Unload package_1 from airplane_0**  
(Action: `unload_airplane package_1 airplane_0 location_1_0`)  
**Timestamp:** 3  
At location_1_0, unload package_1 from airplane_0.  

**Final Goal State achieved:**  
- Package_0 remains at location_1_0 (as per its initial state).  
- Package_1 is now at location_1_0.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, package_1 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0 and package_1 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To transport the specified packages to their target location, follow these steps:

**Step 1: Load package_1 into the airplane**
At location_1_0, load **package_1** into **airplane_0**. Both the package and the airplane are currently at this airport.
*Time: 00:00 - 00:05*

**Step 2: Load package_0 into the airplane**
Still at location_1_0, load **package_0** into **airplane_0**.
*Time: 00:05 - 00:10*

**Step 3: Fly the airplane to the destination city**
Fly **airplane_0** from the airport at **location_1_0** (in city_1) to the airport at **location_0_0** (in city_0).
*Time: 00:10 - 01:10*

**Step 4: Unload package_1 at the destination**
Upon arrival at **location_0_0**, unload **package_1** from **airplane_0**. This places the package at its final destination.
*Time: 01:10 - 01:15*

**Step 5: Unload package_0 at the destination**
Finally, unload **package_0** from **airplane_0** at **location_0_0**.
*Time: 01:15 - 01:20*

**Goal Achievement:** Both package_0 and package_1 are now at location_0_0, fulfilling the logistics requirement.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_0_0, airplane_1 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, package_1 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0 and package_1 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan and specification, here are the human-readable instructions to achieve the goal:

**Action 1: Relocate transport aircraft**
- **Action:** Fly **airplane_1** from its current position at **location_0_0** (the airport in city_0) to **location_1_0** (the airport in city_1).
- **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 01:00

**Action 2: Secure cargo**
- **Action:** At **location_1_0**, load **package_1** into **airplane_1**.
- **Precondition Check:** Both the package and the airplane are now at the same location.
- **Timestamp:** 01:00 - 01:15

**Action 3: Transport cargo to destination city**
- **Action:** Fly **airplane_1** (carrying package_1) from **location_1_0** in city_1 back to **location_0_0** in city_0.
- **Timestamp:** 01:15 - 02:15

**Action 4: Deliver cargo**
- **Action:** Unload **package_1** from **airplane_1** at **location_0_0**.
- **Result:** **package_1** is now at its destination (**location_0_0**). Since **package_0** was already at its goal location (**location_1_0**) from the start, the logistics task is complete.
- **Timestamp:** 02:15 - 02:30

**Final Goal Verification:**
- **package_0** is at **location_1_0**: Verified (unchanged from initial state).
- **package_1** is at **location_0_0**: Verified (delivered via airplane_1).

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_1.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan and logistics specification, here is the sequence of actions required to transport the package to its destination:

**Step 1: Load the package**
*   **Action:** Load **package_0** into **truck_1** at **location_1_0**.
*   **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 00:05
*   **Details:** Both the package and the truck are currently at the airport (location_1_0) in city_1. This action secures the package for transport.

**Step 2: Drive to the destination**
*   **Action:** Drive **truck_1** from **location_1_0** to **location_1_1** within **city_1**.
*   **Timestamp:** 00:05 - 00:20
*   **Details:** The truck travels between the two locations within the same city to reach the delivery point.

**Step 3: Unload the package**
*   **Action:** Unload **package_0** from **truck_1** at **location_1_1**.
*   **Timestamp:** 00:20 - 00:25
*   **Details:** Once the truck arrives at the destination, the package is removed from the vehicle and placed at location_1_1, completing the goal.

**Final Goal Status:** Package_0 is now at location_1_1.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_0_1, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_1_1, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To transport package_0 from its starting position at location_1_1 to the final destination at location_0_0, please follow these instructions:

**Step 1: Load package into truck**  
At location_1_1 in city_1, load **package_0** into **truck_1**.  
*Timestamp: 00:00*

**Step 2: Drive truck to the airport**  
Drive **truck_1** from its current position (location_1_1) to **location_1_0** (the airport for city_1).  
*Timestamp: 00:10*

**Step 3: Unload package from truck**  
Once at the airport (location_1_0), unload **package_0** from **truck_1**.  
*Timestamp: 00:20*

**Step 4: Load package into airplane**  
Still at location_1_0, load **package_0** into **airplane_0**.  
*Timestamp: 00:25*

**Step 5: Fly to the destination city**  
Fly **airplane_0** from the city_1 airport (location_1_0) to the city_0 airport (**location_0_0**).  
*Timestamp: 00:35*

**Step 6: Unload package at destination**  
Finally, at location_0_0, unload **package_0** from **airplane_0**. The package is now at its intended destination.  
*Timestamp: 01:00*
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_1, package_1 is at location_0_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0 and package_1 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having package_0 at location_1_0 and package_1 at location_0_0, follow these steps:

**Step 1: Load package_0 into truck_1**
- **Action:** Load package_0 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
- **Timestamp:** 08:00 AM
- **Details:** Since both package_0 and truck_1 are currently at location_1_1, the package is loaded into the truck. package_0 is no longer at the location and is now inside truck_1.

**Step 2: Drive truck_1 to the airport**
- **Action:** Drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_0 in city_1.
- **Timestamp:** 08:10 AM
- **Details:** truck_1 (containing package_0) is driven from location_1_1 to location_1_0, which serves as the airport for city_1.

**Step 3: Unload package_0 from truck_1**
- **Action:** Unload package_0 from truck_1 at location_1_0.
- **Timestamp:** 08:30 AM
- **Details:** Upon arriving at the airport (location_1_0), package_0 is unloaded from the truck. package_0 is now at location_1_0, and package_1 remains at its initial position of location_0_0, satisfying the final goal.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_0_0, package_1 is at location_0_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_1 and package_1 is at location_0_1.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of transporting **package_0** and **package_1** to **location_0_1**, the following steps must be completed:

**Step 1: Load Package 1**
At **location_0_0**, load **package_1** into **truck_0**. This is possible as both the package and the truck are currently at this location.

**Step 2: Load Package 0**
Remaining at **location_0_0**, load **package_0** into **truck_0**. Now, both packages are secured within the truck.

**Step 3: Drive to Destination**
Drive **truck_0** from **location_0_0** to **location_0_1**. Since both locations are within **city_0**, this transport is permitted. 

**Step 4: Unload Package 1**
Upon arrival at **location_0_1**, unload **package_1** from **truck_0**. The package is now at its target location.

**Step 5: Unload Package 0**
Finally, at **location_0_1**, unload **package_0** from **truck_0**. 

**Goal Status:** Both **package_0** and **package_1** have been successfully delivered to **location_0_1**.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_0_0, package_1 is at location_1_1, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_1 and package_1 is at location_1_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
